# Who's rewriting what (the Ren'Py claims register)

Several sessions rewrite catalog routes into Ren'Py at once. **Claim a route here before you start
it** (plan, art or script), and update the row when its status changes. One route per branch and
worktree (`catalog-renpy-<route>`); the shared files (`game/catalog/`, `screens.rpy`) change from
one session at a time. Session names are the ones `ListAgents` shows. Times are US Eastern.
Message the session that holds a claim (`SendMessage`) before you touch its route, and drop a
claim you won't do by deleting its row.

Status words: **plan** (drafted, awaiting sign-off), **signed off**, **script** (being written),
**built** (script done, walks pass, art pending or partial), **art** (pictures generating),
**merged** (on main).

## Worktrees are slimmed (twinetesting-87, 2026-11-12 ~11 pm ET)

Every `catalog-renpy-<route>` worktree checked out all of `game/art` (~217 MB each, ~20 GB over 96
worktrees). `tools/slim_worktree.py` puts each worktree on a sparse checkout that keeps everything
except **other routes' art**: your own route's `game/art/<route>/` and `game/art/<route>.<set>/`
stay, as does every route's `cover.jpg` (the main menu draws them). Nothing else changes: `git
status`, commits, merges and `sync_art.py` work as before (excluded files are skip-worktree, not
deleted from git). A new worktree: `python3 tools/slim_worktree.py <path>` after `git worktree add`.
Undo for one worktree: `python3 tools/slim_worktree.py --undo <path>`. If your route's folder
name differs from its `game/<route>/` directory, pass `--route <id>`.

## New covers for every route (owner, 2026-11-13 about 1:02 am ET, told to twinetesting-c9)

"When all claims are done and scripts for them finished, make a new cover for each story. This takes priority over other image
gen." Settled the same minute (the owner's answers):

- **Every route, each chat its own.** Each chat writes a cover prompt for every route it owns: the route's signature sex act with its
  lead partner, mid-motion (the owner's 2026-11-09 ruling). It goes in as the `cover` vn entry in `routes/<route>.json`.
- **3 candidates each, Vanripper only.** Eye-check them at full size and pick one. Land it, then copy it to
  `catalog/imagepacks/_custom/ui/route-covers/<route>.jpg` (sync_art reads that file).
- **When:** once every claimed route's script is built. bc's five packages are the last ones being written. Then the cover jobs go to
  the head of the shared queue, and **the job running at that moment is paused**: stopped, and resumed afterwards with only its
  missing scenes.
- **Rows:** each chat adds its cover jobs to the queue table below, as usual.

order, and **every queued or running image job gets a row here naming the chat that queued it**.

twinetesting-32's logs are in its scratchpad (its session scratchpad). Its passes
waiting on m8 start together when m8 ends and take turns at the slot.

twinetesting-92's passes run one at a time from a queue runner that never stops on its own: `route-covers/pipeline/nq92/runner.sh`, with the jobs as the files in `nq92/` and the log in `nq92/runner.log`. It moved there from the session scratchpad at 1:29 pm ET on 2026-11-12, because a session restart wiped the scratchpad and the queue sat idle from about 1:13 to 1:29 pm. It logs "QUEUE EMPTY" when it runs out, and twinetesting-92 messages the other chats then (owner, 2026-11-12: "signal to the other chats when you are done with the image work"). Stop it with `touch nq92/STOP`.
